- **impugnar-pericia**: Elabora impugnação técnica a laudo pericial judicial previdenciário. Ataca cada brecha identificada (vícios formais, omissões, insuficiência de exame, desconsideração de documentos, conclusão desproporcional), pede esclarecimentos do perito ou nova perícia com especialista. Consome saída de [[analisar-laudo-pericial]]. Use sempre que o advogado disser: "impugnar laudo", "impugnação à perícia", "laudo errado", "perito errou", "vou contestar o laudo", "manifestação sobre laudo", "pedir nova perícia". Não use pra analisar laudo (use [[analisar-laudo-pericial]] antes). ([raphaelRPC/dr-claudio](https://github.com/raphaelRPC/dr-claudio/tree/HEAD/familias/dr-claudio/dr-claudio-previdenciario-judicial/skills/impugnar-pericia) · ★11)
- **impugnar-pericia-empresa**: Impugnação técnica a laudo pericial favorável ao trabalhador. Ataca brechas (omissão dos documentos da empresa, desconsideração dos EPIs documentados, exame superficial, CID divergente sem justificativa, conclusão sem fundamentação científica). Pede esclarecimentos ou nova perícia. Use sempre que disser: "impugnar laudo favorável trabalhador", "laudo perdeu causa", "perito ignorou nossa documentação", "perícia desfavorável empresa". Não use pra quesitos (use [[quesitos-pericia-empresa]]). ([raphaelRPC/dr-claudio](https://github.com/raphaelRPC/dr-claudio/tree/HEAD/familias/dr-claudio/dr-claudio-trabalhista-passivo/skills/impugnar-pericia-empresa) · ★11)
- **impugnar-pericia-trabalhador**: Elabora impugnação técnica a laudo pericial trabalhista desfavorável — ataca cada brecha (vícios formais, omissões, desconsideração de documentos, conclusão desproporcional ao exame, EPI declarado eficaz sem prova). Pede esclarecimentos, nova perícia com especialista ou nulidade. Use sempre que o advogado disser: "impugnar laudo", "laudo desfavorável", "perito não viu meu cliente direito", "pedir nova perícia", "manifestação sobre laudo desfavorável". Não use pra elaborar quesitos (use [[quesitos-pericia-trabalhador]]). ([raphaelRPC/dr-claudio](https://github.com/raphaelRPC/dr-claudio/tree/HEAD/familias/dr-claudio/dr-claudio-trabalhista-ativo/skills/impugnar-pericia-trabalhador) · ★11)

- **imputation-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a new or existing scRNA-seq imputation method needs head-to-head evaluation against published imputers on simulated or real developmental datasets, scored by downstream pseudotime, clustering, or correlation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-benchmark-comparison--976120ddcf66f10f))
- **imputation-correlation-analysis-vs-measured-proteins**: Use when training and test single-cell multi-omic data are not perfectly cell-type aligned or come from different tissues/protocols (PBMC vs CBMC, CITE-seq vs REAP-seq), and per-protein imputation accuracy must be quantified against measured protein levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-correlation-analysis-vs-measured-proteins))
- **imputation-design**: Designs a missing-value imputation strategy — missingness-mechanism diagnosis (MCAR/MAR/MNAR), method selection (deletion / mean-median-mode / KNN / MICE / model-based / domain / time-aware), the missingness-indicator decision, fit-on-train-only mechanics, and a post-imputation distortion check. Use when a dataset has nulls and you must decide how to fill them before training, when mean-imputation is distorting variance or correlations, or when missingness itself may be informative. Defers heavy temporal gap-fill to /timeseries-resample and leakage enforcement to /leakage-audit. ([gmanch94/claude-code-aidlc-template](https://github.com/gmanch94/claude-code-aidlc-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/imputation-design) · ★2)
- **imputation-error-quantification**: Use when comparing missing-value imputation methods on complete benchmark data where values can be removed completely at random (10%, 20%, 30%) and true values are known, so NRMSE (continuous) and PFC (categorical) error rates can be computed per simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-error-quantification))
- **imputation-flag-annotation**: Use when a wide-format feature table (samples as columns, features as rows) contains missing entries that must be filled before downstream statistics, and each imputed feature needs a binary indicator flag for provenance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-flag-annotation))
- **imputation-method-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing how much different imputation strategies (scImpute, drImpute, MAGIC, AutoImpute) improve cell-type clustering of sparse scRNA-seq count matrices, measured by ARI against original cell annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-benchmark-comparison))
- **imputation-method-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating whether an imputation method recovers cell-type-specific signal in sparse scATAC-seq data, e.g. comparing SCALE's adata.obsm['imputed'] matrix against scImpute, SAVER, MAGIC or scVI via meta-cell correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-benchmarking))
- **imputation-method-benchmarking**: Use when selecting a missing value imputation method for MNAR left-censored metabolomics (LOD/LOQ-caused) data, or when building an evaluation pipeline that must rank candidate imputers by TPR, RMSE, or SOR metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-method-benchmarking))
- **imputation-method-benchmarking**: Use when choosing among imputation strategies for omics data with sporadic missingness (missing completely at random in individual entries), or when complete-case analysis is infeasible and bias of downstream clustering estimates must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/imputation-method-benchmarking))
- **imputation-method-benchmarking**: Use when comparing whether an imputation method improves downstream clustering quality over raw data and competitor imputers on real scRNA-seq datasets, using clustering-quality indices and significance tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-benchmarking))
- **imputation-method-benchmarking-across-datasets**: Use when raw scRNA-Seq count matrices with known cell-type labels are available and you need to quantify whether imputation improves downstream clustering versus raw data and control methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-benchmarking-across-datasets))
- **imputation-method-comparison**: Use when comparing runtime/scaling of scRNA-seq imputation methods on large datasets (tens of thousands of cells), e.g. to decide which imputer is tractable for &gt;10,000-cell matrices on a single core. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-comparison))
- **imputation-method-comparison-across-datasets**: Use when you have imputed scRNA-seq matrices from multiple methods on log10(count+1) scale plus ground truth, and need to visually compare which methods recover nonlinear gene-gene patterns for selected gene pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-comparison-across-datasets))
- **imputation-method-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare the computational scalability of scRNA-seq imputation methods across increasing cell counts (e.g. 5000–50000 cells) and report a runtime-vs-cell-count table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-method-runtime-benchmarking))
- **imputation-mse-assessment-against-true-counts**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-Seq imputation/denoising methods on simulated data where a ground-truth count matrix exists, and you need a quantitative recovery metric (MSE) plus cell-structure recovery (ASW) per noise type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-mse-assessment-against-true-counts))
- **imputation-ordering-robustness-evaluation**: Use when an imputation method claims arrangement-invariant recovery of scRNA-seq expression values and you must verify that permuted cell/gene orderings yield near-identical imputation correlations with minimal accuracy loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-ordering-robustness-evaluation))
- **imputation-output-consistency-checking**: Use when a random-forest (missForest) imputation has been run on CBCL behavioral item data in the Nextflow multi-omics workflow and the output matrix must be checked before feeding it to the FactoMineR MCA step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-output-consistency-checking))
- **imputation-performance-metrics-calculation**: Use when when you have imputed a metabolomics dataset using multiple MNAR (missing not at random) imputation methods and possess the ground-truth values (from simulation or manual retrieval). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/imputation-performance-metrics-calculation))
- **imputation-qc**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Genomics/phasing-imputation/imputation-qc) · ★7)
- **imputation-quality-evaluation**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data with ERCC spike-in transcripts of known concentrations are available and you need to verify that scImpute imputation recovers true spike-in expression rather than introducing bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-quality-evaluation))
- **imputation-quality-validation**: Use when after applying BPCA imputation to a filtered metabolite matrix (with metabolites having &gt;80% missingness already removed) and before proceeding to median normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/imputation-quality-validation))
- **imputation-recovery-error-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking an imputation method on scRNA-seq count matrices: you need controlled dropout recovery curves (error vs masking percentage) comparable to Fig. 3 of the AutoImpute paper. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-recovery-error-metric-computation))
- **imputation-result-table-compilation**: Use when multiple scRNA-seq imputation methods have been run on identical masked inputs and you must quantify imputation accuracy as MSE against ground truth, or gtMSE when no ground truth exists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-result-table-compilation))
- **imputation-rmse-evaluation**: Use when imputation accuracy must be quantified on data where true values are known (simulated or masked complete matrices) and missingness combines MNAR below-LOD values with MAR dropouts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-rmse-evaluation))
- **imputation-robustness-analysis-across-dropout-scenarios**: Use when comparing scRNA-seq imputation methods for robustness across dropout rates, normalization techniques, or quantification scenarios — e.g. Splatter-simulated negative-binomial datasets where ARI against known cell-type labels must be aggregated per method and scenario. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-robustness-analysis-across-dropout-scenarios))
- **imputation-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational cost of imputation algorithms (KNN-EU vs KNN-CR vs KNN-TN) on an identical metabolomics dataset, or when quantifying the runtime penalty of truncated-normal MLE estimation before choosing an imputer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-runtime-benchmarking))
- **imputation-signal-preservation-evaluation**: Use when comparing an imputed scRNA-seq matrix to its original counterpart to quantify whether imputation preserved the dominant biological structure captured by the principal components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputation-signal-preservation-evaluation))
- **imputation-stopping-criterion-design**: Use when building or tuning an iterative imputation loop (e.g. missForest) and you need a criterion to terminate iterations without a test set — e.g. a masked n x p matrix with 10%-30% MCAR values and mixed continuous/categorical columns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputation-stopping-criterion-design))
- **imputed-accessibility-matrix-handling**: Use when a raw, sparse scATAC-seq peak matrix has been denoised with SCALE and the task requires the numerical imputed matrix (adata.obsm['imputed']) as input to downstream tools like chromVAR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/imputed-accessibility-matrix-handling))
- **imputed-matrix-completeness-validation**: Use when a sparse spot-by-poly(A) site RUD matrix has been imputed and you must confirm no missing entries remain and that imputed values preserve the known spatial structure (e.g., MOB layers). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputed-matrix-completeness-validation))
- **imputed-merfish-transcriptome-analysis**: Use when you have a SpatialScope-implemented (imputed) image-based ST dataset such as MERFISH mouse1_slice180 with paired snRNA-seq cell type labels and want cell-type-specific or global spatially DE genes beyond the original gene panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputed-merfish-transcriptome-analysis))
- **imputed-omics-data-handling**: Use when transcriptomics or metabolomics matrices contain missing values before MOFA integration in BiomiX, and a decision must be made between imputing (NIPALS, random forest, lasso, mean/median, 0) or passing unimputed data to MOFA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/imputed-omics-data-handling))
- **imputed-vs-raw-scrnaseq-comparison**: Use when you have raw scRNA-seq counts plus one or more imputed versions of the same matrix (e.g. scGCL, GraphSCI h5/h5ad outputs) and want to judge imputation quality by DEG patterns and disease-pathway recovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/imputed-vs-raw-scrnaseq-comparison))
- **imputing-proteomics-data**: Impute missing values in protein-level proteomics data matrices. Use when (1) preparing proteomics data for downstream analyses requiring complete matrices (PCA, batch correction), (2) evaluating whether imputation is needed, (3) selecting appropriate imputation methods, or (4) assessing imputation quality. Does NOT cover normalization or batch correction. ([MannLabs/proteomics-agent-skills](https://github.com/MannLabs/proteomics-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/proteomics/skills/imputing-proteomics-data) · ★17)
- **IMRAD Research Paper Scripting**: Creates engaging, step-by-step video scripts explaining the 17 parts of a research paper in IMRAD format, tailored for animation and AI voiceover. ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/english_gpt4_8_GLM4.7/imrad-research-paper-scripting) · ★620 · 5 copies)
- **IMRAD Structure**: Comprehensive guidance on structuring medical research manuscripts using the Introduction-Methods-Results-and-Discussion format, with section-specific writing strategies and common pitfalls. ([ProFlow-Labs-Ai/medsyniq-lite](https://github.com/ProFlow-Labs-Ai/medsyniq-lite/tree/HEAD/skills/medical-writing/imrad-structure) · ★1)
- **imrad-academic**: IMRaD 학술 논문 구조. 연구 보고서, 논문, 학술 자료, 실험 보고를 작성할 때 사용합니다. '논문', '연구', '학술', '실험', '분석 보고서' 요청 시 적용. ([videa-edtech/careerone-chatbot](https://github.com/videa-edtech/careerone-chatbot/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/imrad-academic) · 2 copies)
- **imrad-intro**: Use when the user writes /intro or /imrad, mentions writing an academic introduction, marco teórico, or "R1" for an engineering class report, or shares physics/engineering topics alongside a request for academic text. Primary skill for IMRaD-format scientific introductions in Spanish for university engineering courses (Tec de Monterrey and equivalents). ([ElHakerMiguelito/imrad-intro-skill](https://github.com/ElHakerMiguelito/imrad-intro-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **imrad-structure**: Use when drafting, restructuring, or reviewing an empirical scientific paper. Covers the IMRaD format (Introduction, Methods, Results, Discussion) — what each section contains, the rhetorical move set, common failure modes, and section-by-section editing checks. ([aadarwal/plugins](https://github.com/aadarwal/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/paper/science-writing/skills/imrad-structure))
- **imrad-thesis-writer**: Drafts full IMRaD theses and dissertations with strict cross-chapter non-redundancy, keeping contributions only in the Introduction, technical detail only in Methods, neutral reporting in Results, and interpretation in Discussion. Use when scaffolding or revising a multi-chapter thesis, dissertation, or long-form research monograph. ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/writing-citation/imrad-thesis-writer))
- **imreview**: Review a PR or local changes with InferMatrixCopilot Direct mode. Use when the user invokes imreview. ([InferMatrixLab/InferMatrixCopilot](https://github.com/InferMatrixLab/InferMatrixCopilot/tree/HEAD/plugins/infermatrix-copilot/skills/imreview) · ★18)
- **imrfc**: Create, import, publish, and track RFCs through InferMatrixCopilot's portable RFC service, including implementation evidence, acceptance criteria, and next actions. - **IMS Knowledge Base**: This skill should be used when the user asks about IMS, DL/I calls, hierarchical databases, segments, DBD/PSB definitions, or when COBOL programs containing CALL CBLTDLI or EXEC DLI appear in conversation. - **ims-application**: &gt; ⚠ **SKILL INTEGRITY — NEVER REMOVE OR SHORTEN** &gt; Content in this file may only be **added to or updated**. - **ims-ccs-distribution-comparison**: Use when running AutoCCS single-field DTIMS calibration on large multi-run datasets (e.g. 7106 RapidFire SPE-IMS-MS runs) and you need to evaluate whether per-run pressure/temperature corrections reduce CCS variance for a target ion such as protonated rotenone. - **ims-dataset-quality-filtering**: Use when curating a large batch of public METASPACE IMS datasets (e.g., before training or benchmarking a metabolite annotation model) and you need to exclude non-centroided or low-quality profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ims-dataset-quality-filtering))
- **ims-drift-time-array-handling**: Use when raw IMS-MS data in MZA (HDF5) format must be extracted with the drift-time dimension intact — e.g. building arrival time distributions or precursor arrays retaining mz, intensity, retention time, and drift time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ims-drift-time-array-handling))
- **ims-generator**: Generates Interaction Metadata Schema (IMS v1) annotations for interactive web applications, ensuring every UI interaction has proper metadata for testing and agent execution ([automata-network/claude-plugins](https://github.com/automata-network/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/frontend-testing/skills/ims-generator))
- **ims-imc-image-alignment**: Use when paired TOF-SIMS (metabolite) and IMC (protein) images of the same tissue FOV need geometric alignment before single-cell segmentation or joint protein-metabolite analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ims-imc-image-alignment))
- **ims-rollup**: Use when rolling up &gt;=2 component schedules into one program IMS view: program milestones, slip deltas, critical cross-links, one ASK. NOT for single-project Gantt, not program-roadmap capability tranches, not critical-path this week alone, not interdependency-map, not milestone-burn, not tsa-schedule. ([mmoranodevelop/hard-half](https://github.com/mmoranodevelop/hard-half/tree/HEAD/skills/project/ims-rollup) · ★4)
- **ims-spatial-intensity-visualization**: Use when you have imzML/HDF5 MALDI-MSI data and need to visualize the spatial distribution of a specific m/z peak — e.g. to verify that a peak correlated with a tumor class is genuinely elevated in the annotated tumor region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ims-spatial-intensity-visualization))
- **imsa**: Query the IMSA DuckDB dataset (output/imsa.duckdb). Includes schema guidance for seasons and laps plus formatting macros. Use for analytics, session lookups, and lap-level queries. ([tobi/imsa_data](https://github.com/tobi/imsa_data/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/imsa) · ★10)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-fragmentation-simulation-validation) · 2 copies)
- **in-silico-fragmentation-spectrum-prediction**: Use when benchmarking tandem MS metabolite annotation tools: test spectra with known structures must be ranked against a compiled compound database using predicted fragmentation or fingerprints, and top-k retrieval rates compared to ChemDistiller. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-fragmentation-spectrum-prediction))
- **in-silico-fragmentation-tool-benchmarking**: Use when you have SMILES of held-out compounds plus their experimental MS/MS spectra and need to objectively compare whole-spectrum predictions from multiple in silico fragmentation tools (e.g., SingleFrag vs CFM-ID vs 3DMolMS). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-fragmentation-tool-benchmarking))
- **in-silico-gene-knockout-perturbation**: Use when you have a trained CITRUS model output (hid_tmr TF activities), a binary SGA alteration matrix, and want to predict which TFs are associated with specific somatic alterations per cancer type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-gene-knockout-perturbation))
- **in-silico-gene-knockout-simulation**: Use when you have mock and infected context-specific FBA models with a viral biomass objective function and need to rank host genes/gene pairs as antiviral targets by essentiality-vs-safety trade-off. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-gene-knockout-simulation))
- **in-silico-gene-perturbation-simulation**: Use when you have a trained PRESCIENT model and a serialized data_pt torch object and need to predict how overexpression or knockdown of specific genes (TFs or non-TFs) alters terminal cell-fate proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-gene-perturbation-simulation))
- **in-silico-genome-evolution-simulation**: Use when you need to benchmark a strain-tracking or genome-comparison tool's sensitivity to specific mutation types (SNPs vs indels) by simulating controlled in silico evolution and comparing pairwise score distributions across mutation regimes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-genome-evolution-simulation))
- **in-silico-genome-sv-injection**: Use when benchmarking somatic SV calling methods (e.g. nanomonsv vs detection-subtraction or CAMPHORsomatic) requires ground-truth breakpoints and controlled tumor purity/depth scenarios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-genome-sv-injection))
- **in-silico-lc-ms-simulation**: Use when developing or comparing MS2 acquisition/DDA prioritization strategies on complex metabolite mixtures and you need to evaluate a scan schedule (coverage of picked peaks) in silico from a full-scan mzML before instrument time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-lc-ms-simulation))
- **in-silico-lipid-fragmentation-spectra-handling**: Use when building a targeted LC-MS/MS lipid identification workflow that needs a spectral library of lipid species (defined by class, fatty acid composition, and adduct) exported as '.MSP', or an accompanying precursor/transition list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-lipid-fragmentation-spectra-handling))
- **in-silico-lipid-library-search**: Use when per-feature lipid annotations are needed for Thermo mzML lipidomics data via MS/MS spectral matching in MAVEN2 against the CalicoLipids and MS-DIAL in-silico libraries scored by hypergeometric score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-lipid-library-search))
- **in-silico-mass-spectral-library-construction**: Use when an experimental spectral library (e.g., NIST 2017 with only 0.27 million EI spectra) lacks coverage and you need large-scale predicted spectra from molecular structures to support compound identification by spectrum matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-mass-spectral-library-construction))
- **in-silico-mass-spectrum-generation**: Use when you need to predict an MS/MS spectrum for a small molecule lacking library spectra — e.g. a metabolite, gut-microbiome compound, or any structure whose experimental CID-MS/MS spectrum is absent — and you can afford quantum-chemistry MD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-mass-spectrum-generation))
- **in-silico-metabolism-tool-orchestration**: Use when benchmarking or comparing in silico metabolism prediction software on a common substrate set, e.g. to reproduce baseline precision/recall/Jaccard ranges before evaluating a new predictor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-metabolism-tool-orchestration))
- **in-silico-metabolite-annotation**: Use when you have .mgf MS/MS spectra (e.g. [M+H]+ adducts, parent masses 100–1500 Da, ≥6 fragments) and need candidate molecular formula/structure annotations for dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-metabolite-annotation))
- **in-silico-metabolite-biotransformation-generation**: Use when a knowledge-driven metabolic reaction network built from KEGG, MetaCyc, and HMDB is too sparse, and putative metabolites/reaction pairs absent from those databases are needed to improve annotation coverage and topological connectivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-metabolite-biotransformation-generation))
- **in-silico-methylome-mixture-simulation**: Use when you need to quantify methylated vs non-methylated motif-site fractions from SMALR SMsn scores without applying thresholds — e.g., estimating native fraction in native/WGA mixtures or probing the coverage-versus-accuracy tradeoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-methylome-mixture-simulation))
- **in-silico-mixture-deconvolution-validation**: Use when validating mixture-deconvolution performance on in silico scRNA-seq mixtures of known composition (5-9 contributors) where the ground-truth contributor count can be compared against the number of deconvoluted cell clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-mixture-deconvolution-validation))
- **in-silico-mixture-generation-from-reference-profiles**: Use when benchmarking a methylation deconvolution tool (e.g., DXM) and ground-truth subpopulation profiles and prevalences are needed that cannot be obtained from real mixed samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-mixture-generation-from-reference-profiles))
- **in-silico-msms-annotation**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS data in .mzML format contains features that cannot be annotated by spectral matching alone, and per-feature chemical structures, formulas, and chemical classes must be assigned and ranked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-annotation))
- **in-silico-msms-fragment-prediction**: Use when building a spectral library for adductomics or metabolomics where reference standards are unavailable and in silico MS/MS fragments are needed for each structure entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-fragment-prediction))
- **in-silico-msms-fragmentation-enumeration**: Use when annotating a tandem MS spectrum against candidate molecules whose structures are known, and you need pre-calculated fragmentation patterns or per-candidate fragment scores to rank metabolite annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-fragmentation-enumeration))
- **in-silico-msms-spectral-prediction**: Use when validating putative unknown metabolite annotations from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics (e.g., KGMN/MetDNA2 unknown peaks in NIST urine data) where no spectral library match or chemical standard exists and computational fragmentation support is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-spectral-prediction--e9aef1729a72f09c))
- **in-silico-msms-spectral-prediction**: Use when you need simulated MS/MS spectra for compounds lacking reference library entries, or when benchmarking fragmentation-prediction quality across NIST/MS-DIAL, MSnLib, and CASMI test sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-spectral-prediction--f41ec3a12083f294))
- **in-silico-msms-spectrum-prediction**: Use when a large set of candidate metabolite structures needs predicted ESI tandem mass spectra in both ionization modes so experimental MS/MS spectra can be matched to structures without experimental reference libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/in-silico-msms-spectrum-prediction))
- **in-silico-mutagenesis-perturbation-prediction**: Use when enhancer–gene assignments must be validated against CRISPR perturbation measurements and a sequence-model counterfactual (predicted expression change after enhancer removal) is needed as an alternative to activity-by-contact scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-mutagenesis-perturbation-prediction))
- **in-silico-mutagenesis-strain-generation**: Use when benchmarking strain-variant discovery (e.g. MetaQuad) and you need simulated shotgun metagenomic samples with known strain composition, planted substitutions, and controlled mean depths instead of real clinical reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-mutagenesis-strain-generation))
- **in-silico-neuromarketing-platform-pattern**: The emerging pattern of AI-powered neuromarketing platforms that replace physical fMRI/EEG testing with computational brain-activity prediction using open foundation models (Meta TRIBE v2), enabling privacy-first neural engagement scoring at 1/1000th the cost of clinical trials. Use when evaluating neuromarketing tooling, designing privacy-first content testing pipelines, or building neural engagement scoring into A-Tech products (A-Coder cognitive-load prediction, Be Practical content optimization, Builder's Club community engagement analytics). ([hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library](https://github.com/hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library/tree/HEAD/marketing-and-content/in-silico-neuromarketing-platform-pattern))
- **in-silico-pcr-amplicon-generation**: Use when comparing taxonomic resolution of short-read 16S sub-region amplicons against full-length 16S, and you need sub-region FASTA files excised from full-length reference sequences using real PCR primer locations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/in-silico-pcr-amplicon-generation))
- **in-silico-pcr-primer-matching**: Use when a set of primer pairs (designed or published) must be scored for detection of a curated reference gene set — e.g., computing what fraction of 720 amoA-AOB target genes are amplified in silico, or comparing coverage between two primer sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-pcr-primer-matching))
- **in-silico-perturbation**: Run an in-silico gene knockout, knockdown or overexpression screen with Helical foundation models by editing the expression matrix locally, then embedding the perturbed cells on the platform. Use when the user wants to perturb genes, screen targets, or find genes whose perturbation shifts diseased cells toward a healthy state — even when no perturbation tool is available, because none is needed. ([MaybeBio/Daily-Code-Agent-VirtualCell](https://github.com/MaybeBio/Daily-Code-Agent-VirtualCell/tree/HEAD/code/helicalAI__heli-plugin/code/plugins/helical-platform/skills/in-silico-perturbation) · 2 copies)
- **in-silico-perturbation-oracle**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/in-silico-perturbation-oracle) · ★745)
- **in-silico-perturbation-oracle**: Virtual gene knockout simulation using foundation models to predict transcriptional changes ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/in-silico-perturbation-oracle))
- **in-silico-protease-digestion-of-orf-proteome**: Use when a long-read isoform catalog yields predicted ORF proteins and you need to enumerate which tryptic peptides are novel relative to a reference proteome (e.g., UniProt mouse) before targeted mass spectrometry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-protease-digestion-of-orf-proteome))
- **in-silico-read-abundance-mixing**: Use when benchmarking metagenomic assembly of strain-diverse viral communities requires a controlled ground truth: target-organism reads must be spiked into a complex background at a known abundance before assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-read-abundance-mixing))
- **in-silico-read-mixture-simulation**: Use when you need to quantify how reliably an AMP-based predictor (Mykrobe) detects a low-frequency contaminant or resistant subpopulation in NGS reads, e.g. down to 0.5–2% minor-population frequency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/in-silico-read-mixture-simulation))
- **in-silico-read-simulation**: Use when benchmarking methylation read aligners (e.g., Msuite vs Bismark vs BWA-meth) on ground-truth data, or when stratifying mapping performance by region composition such as CT/GA-rich bins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/in-silico-read-simulation))
